# Python API reference

Generated by `python -I scripts/docs/build_reference.py` from the source with `ast` (nothing is imported). Public means a name without a leading underscore. Do not edit by hand (ROADMAP B6, T1167). Task cadence and commands are in [tasks.md](tasks.md).

409 modules, 3329 public functions, 97 classes; 1793 have a docstring.

## `pipeline/ai_gateway.py`

The pipeline's one way to call a language model (T1137, ROADMAP E3).

| Kind | Name | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| class | `AIRefusal` | A refused or failed call: code is one of AI_CODES, status its HTTP status. |
| class | `AIResult` |  |
| def | `resolve_config(feature, env=None, models=None)` | The resolved config for a feature, with cfg["error"] set when it may not run. |
| def | `should_fall_over(status)` |  |
| def | `attempt_timeout_s(index, elapsed_s, cfg)` |  |
| def | `parse_model_json(text, loose=False)` | A model's JSON answer, or None. loose also takes a fenced block or the outermost {...}. |
| def | `strip_invisible(s)` |  |
| def | `fold_for_scan(s)` |  |
| def | `scan_injection(text)` | {'hit': bool, 'rules': [ids]}: instruction-shaped text, on the folded text and with leetspeak and spacing undone. |
| def | `prompt_shingles(system_text, n=6)` |  |
| def | `scan_output_severe(text, shingles=None)` | The severe output issues of aiGuard.scanOutput: canary, leak, template, hijack. |
| class | `Gateway` | One job's model access: its config, the rate floor between calls and token counters. |
| method | `Gateway.call(self, user, *, system='', json=False, schema=None, search=False, temperature=0.2, max_tokens=8192, screen=None)` | One call under the rules in the module docstring. Returns AIResult or raises AIRefusal. |

## `pipeline/apply_overrides.py`

Hand corrections to the catalogue, applied last in every pipeline run.

| Kind | Name | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| class | `OverrideError` | An overrides file breaks a rule; the message names file, entry and rule. |
| def | `entry_problems(entry)` | The rules one entry breaks, as short sentences (empty when it is fine). |
| def | `load_layer(root, layer)` | The checked entries of one layer's file ([] when the file is absent). |
| def | `load_all(root)` | {layer: entries} for every layer; raises OverrideError on any bad file. |
| def | `set_path(record, path, value, *, create_leaf=True)` | Set a dotted path in a dict. Returns 'set', 'same' or 'no_parent' (or 'no_leaf' when create_leaf is False and the leaf key is absent). |
| def | `patch_record(record, patch, *, create_leaf=True)` | Apply a patch to one record. Returns {path: outcome}. |
| def | `apply_all(root=ROOT, *, write=True)` | Check every file, then apply every layer. Returns {layer: [result]}. |
| def | `build_snapshot(root=ROOT, results=None, *, now=None)` | What the admin Overrides tab lists: every entry with its layer, file and the outcome of the last apply (or 'not run'). |
| def | `write_snapshot(root=ROOT, results=None)` |  |
| def | `main(argv=None)` |  |

## `pipeline/audit_hero_images.py`

Tier: Scheduled (run_pipeline task hero_audit)

| Kind | Name | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| def | `fold(s)` | Accent- and case-free key, so 'Málaga' matches 'malaga'. |
| def | `readable(file_title)` | 'File:Old_town_of_Sibiu_(cropped).jpg' -> 'Old town of Sibiu (cropped)'. |
| def | `file_title_from_url(url)` | The Commons file behind a thumbnail or original URL, as 'File:Name.jpg'. |
| def | `fetch(api, params)` |  |
| def | `commons_meta(titles, cache, use_cache=True)` | {file title -> {mime,width,height,cats,name}} for up to any number of files, batched 50 at a time and memoised in cache/hero_image_meta.json. |
| def | `dest_km_limit(d)` |  |
| def | `dest_point(d)` |  |
| def | `photo_km(meta, d)` | Km from the destination to the nearest coordinate the file publishes, or None when the file publishes none (about three files in four). |
| def | `classify(file_title, meta, url=None, dest=None)` | Empty list = a usable hero. Otherwise the reasons it is not. |
| def | `check(dests, limit=None, only=None)` |  |
| def | `category_images(cat_title, limit=CAT_LIMIT)` | File titles in a Commons category, or [] when it does not exist. |
| def | `view_candidates(city, limit=CAT_LIMIT)` | Everything the curated view categories hold for this place name. |
| def | `article_images(page_url, city)` | Every file used in the destination's own Wikipedia article. The lead photo is nearly always among them, and everything here is at least about the right place. |
| def | `geosearch_images(lat, lon, radius=8000, limit=40)` | Commons files photographed near the place. Second choice: coordinates are honest but say nothing about whether a file is any good. |
| def | `score_candidate(title, meta, city, country, rank=None, lead=False, dest=None, view=False)` | Higher is better. Nothing here is clever: it prefers big landscape photographs that name the place, from the position an editor already chose. `rank` is the file's position in the article (0 = first), `lead` marks the article's own lead ima |
| def | `lead_image(lang, en_title)` | The lead photo of this place's article in `lang`, as a Commons file title. Editors put a postcard at the top of a town's article; that is the picture we want, and no ranking of ours beats it. |
| def | `best_replacement(d, meta_cache, exclude=(), coords_only=False)` | The best photograph we can find for this destination, or None. |
| def | `fix(dests, report, dry_run=False, limit=None, only=None, force_coord=False)` |  |
| def | `dupes(dests, dry_run=False, only=None)` | One photograph standing for two DIFFERENT places. |
| def | `stats()` |  |
| def | `sheet(report=None)` | A local page that puts every swap side by side, old on the left, new on the right. Written to disk rather than published, because the photos load from upload.wikimedia.org and any hosted page worth its salt blocks that. |
| def | `main()` |  |

| class | `Run` | Execution metadata for one collector or one harvester script. |
| class | `Section` | One chapter of the generated ledger. Rows point at it by id. |
| class | `Source` | One ledger row: an external source and one use of it. |
| class | `WireReview` | One row of the share-alike review: produced work or database extract. |
| def | `sources_for(name: str='', script: str='') -> tuple` | The ledger rows that cover a collector name or a script path. |
| def | `register(cls)` | Register a collector class. Refuses one that has no execution entry or no licence row: the ledger rule enforced at import time, so a new collector without its governance fails `run_all --list`, the tests and CI rather than quietly shipping  |
| def | `load_all() -> dict[str, type]` |  |
| def | `harvester_scripts(root: Path=ROOT) -> list[str]` | Every pipeline/harvest_*.py in the tree, plus the stay layer's fetchers (pipeline/stays/{harvest,scrape,price,discover,calibrate}_*.py), repo-relative, posix. |
| def | `pipeline_tasks(root: Path=ROOT) -> dict[str, dict]` | run_pipeline.py's task table, read by regex (key, cadence, soft), so the RUNS copy of a cadence is checked against the driver that owns it. run_pipeline.py is never imported: importing it runs environment setup. |
| def | `validate(registry: dict \| None=None, harvesters: list[str] \| None=None, root: Path=ROOT) -> list[str]` | Every rule the ledger depends on, as a list of human-readable problems. Pass `registry` and `harvesters` to test the rules against a synthetic roster; by default they are the live collectors and the harvest_*.py files on disk. |
| def | `credit_required(row: 'Source') -> bool` | True when the row's attribution column asks for a credit and the row is live. Retired rows are history; their credit is owed only while the data still ships, which APP_CREDITS covers when it does. |
| def | `uncredited_rows() -> list[str]` | Problems with the credit audit (T1091). Empty means every live credit-required row has an app entry, is covered by one, or is recorded as not shown. |
